**text**　當調整同時出現廢棄路線和并行路線時,調整和重新分配突發事件行為的事件將突然發生到廢棄路線上,而無論主要規則是如何配置的。 下表顯示在路線編輯中涉及的編輯活動及其相應的事件行為:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "When abandoned routes and concurrent routes are both present in the realignment, events with realign and reassign snap event behavior will snap to abandoned routes regardless of how the dominant rules are configured.\nThe following table shows the edit activities involved in the route edit and their corresponding event behaviors:", "section": "Note:"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#6DF

## 路線調整事件行為 · 注:

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-188

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#6DF

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　上述路線調整有以下效果:事件1在調整之日退休,因為其中一部分在編輯部分。然后它分成兩個事件記錄,在調整后路段上分別使用調整日期作為起始日期。由于重置事件行為是在調整事件行為之前應用的,因此活動1中位于調整部分與原事件1分開并劃入廢棄路線的部分。活動1中留在A線的另一部分在調整時將縮短為A線左分支的長度,然后才適用調整Sunp事件行為。當適用調整“Cong事件”行為時,該事件不必改變維持地理位置的措施,因為它不在編輯部分。事件1分為兩個事件。一個事件在A線停留,開始措施0在1號公路上,最后措施17在2號公路上,另一個事件與廢棄路線交錯,開始措施17在2號公路Ab,最后措施30在3號公路Ab。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The route realignment described above has the following effects:\nEvent 1 is retired on the date of realignment because part of it is in the edit section. It then splits into two event records on the postrealignment route with the realignment date as the starting date. As the reassign event behavior is applied before the realign event behavior, the portion of Event 1 that is in the realignment section splits from the original Event 1 and snaps to the abandoned routes. The other portion of Event 1 that stays on Line A is shortened to the length of the left branch of Line A before the realign Snap event behavior is applied. When the realign Snap event behavior is applied, this event does not need to change its measures to maintain geographic location because it is not in the edit section. As a result, Event 1 splits into two events. One event stays on Line A with a start measure of 0 on Route 1 and an end measure of 17 on Route 2. The other event snaps to the abandoned routes with a start measure of 17 on Route 2_Ab and an end measure of 30 on Route 3_Ab.", "section": "Note:"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#6DF

## 路線調整事件行為 · 注:

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-189

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#6DF

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　事件 2 在調整日退出, 因為部分在編輯部分 。 然后它會拆分為兩個事件記錄 。 由于重整事件行為在調整事件行為之前應用重置事件行為, 活動 2 的一部分從原始事件 2 中分離出來, 并劃入廢棄路線 。 活動 2 的另一部分停留在線 A 上, 縮短為 A 線右分支的長度 。 當應用調整 Snap 事件行為時, 該事件不需要改變維持地理位置的措施, 因為不在編輯部分中 。 結果, 事件 2 分成兩個事件 。在A線上停留,開始措施為4號公路上的43個,最后措施為4號公路上的48個。 另一事件與廢棄路線交錯,開始措施為3號公路的30個,最后措施為4號公路的43個。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Event 2 is retired on the date of realignment because part of it is in the edit section. It then splits into two event records on the postrealignment route with the realignment date as the starting date. As the reassign event behavior is applied before the realign event behavior, the portion of Event 2 that is in the realignment section splits from the original Event 2 and snaps to the abandoned routes. The other portion of Event 2 that stays on Line A is shortened to the length of the right branch of Line A before the realign Snap event behavior is applied. When the realign Snap event behavior is applied, this event does not need to change its measures to maintain geographic location because it is not in the edit section. As a result, Event 2 splits into two events. One event stays on Line A with a start measure of 43 on Route 4 and an end measure of 48 on Route 4. The other event snaps to the abandoned routes with a start measure of 30 on Route 3_Ab and an end measure of 43 on Route 4_Ab.", "section": "Note:"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#6DF

## 路線調整事件行為 · 線路網絡路線調整

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-197

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#E90

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　如果選定的網絡是一個線網,您可以對調整區的路線段提供新的措施。新措施和重新校準的下游路線選項確定是否會在調整后的路線線上開辟新的路線。在下面的例子中,1號公路屬于1號線,從1/2000年開始運行。調整定于2005年1月1日進行,將1號公路中心改道到一個新的地點。以下各小節說明了調整措施和重新調整的不同情景。圖表和表格顯示了調整前的路線和事件信息。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "If the selected network is a line network, you can provide new measures to the route segment in the realignment area. The new measures and the Recalibrate route downstream option determine whether new routes will be created on the line of the realigned route.\nIn the following example, Route 1 belongs to Line 1 and is active from 1/1/2000. The realignment is set to occur on 1/1/2005, where the middle of Route 1 is rerouted to a new location. The following subsections illustrate different scenarios of realignment measures and recalibration. The diagrams and tables demonstrate the route and event information before the realignment.", "section": "Route realignment in a line network"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#E90

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

## 路線調整事件行為 · 注:

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-205

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#884

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　在以下所有例子中,調整事件行為和校準事件行為都設置為“保持原樣”。校準事件行為在調整事件行為之前應用,因此重要的是要先核實事件地層校準事件行為配置,然后再檢查“重新校準”路線下游復選框。要審查用于校準的其他事件行為,請參考用于校準主題的“事件行為”。當事件按照“調整原樣行為”配置時,會維持事件的地理位置,但措施可以改變。如果事件跨過調整區域,也會分裂。調整區域內的事件波形已經停止。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "In all of the following examples, both the realign event behavior and the calibrate event behavior are set to Stay Put.\nThe calibrate event behavior is applied before the realign event behavior, so it is important to verify the event feature layer's calibrate event behavior configuration before checking the Recalibrate route downstream check box.\nTo review other event behaviors for calibration, refer to the Event behavior for route calibration topic.\nWhen events are configured with realign Stay Put behavior, the geographic location of the event is maintained, but the measures can change. The event can also split if it crosses the realign region. Portions of events in the realign region are retired.", "section": "Note:"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#884

## 路線調整事件行為 · 調整,不設置新的路線和下游重新校準

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-206

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#E84

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　在此情況下, 校準路線下游復選框會被檢查。 調整從措施6開始, 到措施18結束, 在1號路段, 由于外形變化而增加的長度為4。 路徑1的調整部分不使用新措施, 這樣它可以在調整后保持單調, 沒有創建新的路徑 。 下表顯示路線編輯中的編輯活動及其相應的事件行為 :

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "In this scenario, the Recalibrate route downstream check box is checked. The realignment starts with measure 6 and ends with measure 18 on Route 1, as an additional length of 4 is added due to shape change. The realigned portion of Route 1 does not use new measures, so it can remain monotonically calibrated after realignment, and no new route is created.\nThe following table shows the edit activities involved in the route edit and their corresponding event behaviors:", "section": "Realignment with no new route creation and with downstream recalibration"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#E84

## 路線調整事件行為 · 調整,不設置新的路線和下游重新校準

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-212

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#E84

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　上述路線調整有以下效果: 事件1在調整之日退休,因為其中部分活動在編輯部分。 在調整后路線上創建了新事件,調整日期為起始日期。開始計量對0的修改,最后計量對6的修改,以適應第1號道路的新措施。 活動2完全在編輯部分內,因此在調整之日退休。事件3在調整之日退休,因為它的一部分在編輯部分。在調整之后路線上創建了新的事件,調整日期為起始日期。開始計量值對18的修改,最終計量值對24的修改,以適應第1號道路的新措施。下圖顯示路線和事件。調整后:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The route realignment described above has the following effects:\nEvent 1 is retired on the date of realignment since part of it is in the edit section. A new event is created on the postrealignment route with the realignment date as the starting date. The start measure changes to 0 and the end measure changes to 6 to accommodate the new measures of Route 1.\nEvent 2 is retired on the date of realignment since it is completely within the edit section.\nEvent 3 is retired on the date of realignment since part of it is in the edit section. A new event is created on the postrealignment route with the realignment date as the starting date. The start measure value changes to 18 and the end measure value changes to 24 to accommodate the new measures of Route 1.\nThe following diagram shows the routes and events after realignment:", "section": "Realignment with no new route creation and with downstream recalibration"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#E84

## 路線調整事件行為 · 調整與新路線的創建和下游重新校準

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-219

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#14B

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　在此情況下, 校準路線下游復選框被檢查。 調整從措施6開始, 以措施18結束, 在1號公路上, 增加4個長度, 由于形狀變化而添加。 但是, 1號公路的重新組合部分有新措施, 從0到12。 在這種情況下, 路1在調整后不能保持單一校準, 因此創建了一條新的路線。 新路線名為“ 路New ” 。 自從校準路線下游復選框被檢查以來, Rightalbrat路線下游重新校準, 并取代了1號公路上最初的下游部分。 新路的新措施由調整時間長短決定。 下表顯示路線編輯中的編輯活動及其相應的事件行為:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "In this scenario, the Recalibrate route downstream check box is checked. The realignment starts with measure 6 and ends with measure 18 on Route 1 as an additional length of 4 is added due to shape change. However, the realigned portion of Route 1 has new measures from 0 to 12. In this case, Route 1 cannot remain monotonically calibrated after realignment, so a new route is created. The new route is named RouteNew.\nSince the Recalibrate route downstream check box is checked, RouteNew is recalibrated downstream, and it replaces the original downstream portion of Route 1. The new measures of RouteNew are determined by the length of the realignment.\nThe following table shows the edit activities involved in the route edit and their corresponding event behaviors:", "section": "Realignment with new route creation and with downstream recalibration"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#14B

## 路線調整事件行為 · 調整與新路線的創建和下游重新校準

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-226

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#14B

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　上述路線調整有以下效果:事件1在調整之日退休,因為其中一部分在編輯部分。在調整日期為起始日期的重整1號公路上創建了一個新事件,將調整日期定為調整日期。開始量度值對0的修改,而最終量度值對6的修改,以適應第1號公路的新測量措施。活動2由于完全在編輯部分內,因此在調整日期終止。活動3由于部分在編輯部分內,因此在調整之日退出。由于WatherNew已經取代了第1號公路,位于第3號活動地點,在應用校準停留布置行為時無法創建。下圖顯示了調整之后的路線和事件:下表提供了調整后的事件詳情。:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The route realignment described above has the following effects:\nEvent 1 is retired on the date of realignment since part of it is in the edit section. A new event is created on the postrealignment Route 1 with the realignment date as the starting date. The start measure value changes to 0 and the end measure value changes to 6 to accommodate the new measures of Route 1.\nEvent 2 is retired on the date of realignment since it is completely within the edit section.\nEvent 3 is retired on the date of realignment since part of it is in the edit section. Since RouteNew has replaced Route 1 at the location of Event 3, Event 3 cannot be created when the calibrate Stay Put behavior is applied.\nThe following diagram shows the routes and events after realignment:\nThe following table provides details about the events after realignment:", "section": "Realignment with new route creation and with downstream recalibration"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#14B

## 路線調整事件行為 · 與新路線的創建和不進行下游重新校準的調整

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-232

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#D1C

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　在此情況下, Ralibrat 路下游復選框沒有被選中。 調整從措施6開始, 以措施12結束, 在1號公路上進行。 但是, 調整是使用選定中線的反向方向進行的。 在這種情況下, 一號公路在調整后不能保持單度校準, 因為調整部分被倒轉, 因此新路線就被創建了。 新路線稱為WareNew. RueNew, 起始尺度為12, 因為調整從1號公路上的12號措施開始, 終點措施24, 因為調整的時間長度是12。 由于Regalibrat 下游檢查箱沒有檢查, 1號公路的右部分已經變成一條新的路線, 即1b路。 1b 道路1b的起始尺度是12。因為下游的起點是1號公路上的最初12項措施,終點為18項措施, 因為右臂長度是6。 下面的表格顯示了路線編輯所涉及的編輯活動及其相應的事件行為:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "In this scenario, the Recalibrate route downstream check box is not checked. The realignment starts with measure 6 and ends with measure 12 on Route 1. However, the realignment is done using the reverse direction of the chosen centerlines. In this case, Route 1 cannot remain monotonically calibrated after realignment, as the realigned portion is reversed, so a new route is created. The new route is named RouteNew. RouteNew has a start measure of 12, because the realignment starts with measure 12 on Route 1, and an end measure of 24, because the length of the realignment is 12.\nSince the Recalibrate route downstream check box is not checked, the right portion of Route 1 has become a new route, Route 1b. Route 1b has a start measure of 12, because the downstream starts with the original measure 12 on Route 1, and an end measure of 18, because the length of the right arm is 6.\nThe following table shows the edit activity involved in the route edit and its corresponding event behavior:", "section": "Realignment with new route creation and without downstream recalibration"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#D1C

**text**　上述路線調整有以下效果:事件1在調整之日退休,因為其中部分內容在編輯一節中。在重整后1號路1上創建了一個新事件,將調整日期定為起始日期。開始量度值對0的修改,而最終量度值對6的修改,以適應第1號路的新測量新措施。活動2完全在編輯一節內,因此在調整之日退休。事件3由于部分內容在編輯一節中,所以在調整之日退休。由于1b號路已經取代了1號路,位于第3號事件所在地,在應用校準保持原樣行為時無法創建第3號事件。下圖顯示了調整后的路線和事件:下表提供了隨后事件的詳情。調整:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The route realignment described above has the following effects:\nEvent 1 is retired on the date of realignment since part of it is in the edit section. A new event is created on the postrealignment Route 1 with the realignment date as the starting date. The start measure value changes to 0 and the end measure value changes to 6 to accommodate the new measures of Route 1.\nEvent 2 is retired on the date of realignment since it is completely within the edit section.\nEvent 3 is retired on the date of realignment since part of it is in the edit section. Since Route 1b has replaced Route 1 at the location of Event 3, Event 3 cannot be created when the calibrate Stay Put behavior is applied.\nThe following diagram shows the routes and events after realignment:\nThe following table provides details about the events after realignment:", "section": "Realignment with new route creation and without downstream recalibration"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#D1C

## 路線調整事件行為 · 路線調整,以線路網絡進行線性調整,并開展一系列活動

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-244

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#899

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　以下各節說明在對線網中一條線路上的路線進行重新調整時如何執行事件行為規則,例如,有四條線路位于A線上,從2000年1月1日開始運行。調整定于2005年1月1日從2號公路中部到4號公路中間進行。以下各分節說明了不同回校路線下游和改派為廢棄路線選項的情景。在本節,圖表和表格展示了調整前的路線和事件信息。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The following sections describe how event behavior rules are enforced that span routes when a route on a line in a line network is realigned.\nIn this example, four routes are on Line A and the routes are active from 1/1/2000. The realignment is set to occur on 1/1/2005 from the middle of Route 2 to the middle of Route 4. The following subsections illustrate scenarios with different Recalibrate route downstream and Reassign to abandoned route options. In this section, the graphics and tables demonstrate the route and event information before the realignment.", "section": "Route realignment in a line network with spanning events"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#899

**text**　以下各節介紹在應用事件行為工具運行后,在下游和改派為廢棄路線選項的不同再校準路線設想情景下如何執行事件行為規則。

**original**　{"text": "The following sections describe how event behavior rules are enforced after running the Apply Event Behaviors tool under scenarios with different Recalibrate route downstream and Reassign to abandoned route options.", "section": "Events before route realignment"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#KCQ

## 路線調整事件行為 · 與棄置情景的對齊 在一個線條網絡中 與"保持原位"事件行為 橫跨事件的連續事件

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-254

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#7EB

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　如果選中的網絡是一個線條網絡, 您可以將調整區域的路線部分重新指定到廢棄的路線, 這是一條新線上的新路線。 調整區的任何事件都遵循重分配事件的行為。 如果您想要將事件轉移到廢棄的路線, 請配置用于重置路線編輯類型的 Snap 行為。 在此示例中, 事件會與“ 重新定位 保持事件” 行為、 重新設置 Cast 事件行為和校準“ 保持原狀” 事件行為一起配置。 在此編輯中檢查下游的重新校正路線和被重置為廢棄路線的路徑。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "If the selected network is a line network, you can reassign the route portion in the realignment area to the abandoned route, which is a new route on a new line. Any event in the realign section follows the reassign event behavior. If you want the events to transfer to the abandoned route, configure the Snap behavior for the reassign route editing type.\nIn this example, the events are configured with the realign Stay Put event behavior, reassign Snap event behavior, and calibrate Stay Put event behavior. Both Recalibrate route downstream and Reassign to abandoned route are checked in this edit.", "section": "Realign with abandonment scenario in a line network with spanning events with Stay Put event behavior"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#7EB

## 路線調整事件行為 · 注:

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-255

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#7EB

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　在調整事件行為之前應用重新指派事件的行為, 所以在檢查重置為廢棄路由復選框前必須先驗證事件特性層重新指派事件的行為配置。 校準事件行為在調整事件行為前應用, 因此在檢查回校路徑下游復選框前必須驗證事件特性層校準事件的行為配置。 要探索用于重置和校準的其他事件行為, 請參考路由重整的事件行為和路由校準主題的事件行為。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The reassign event behavior is applied before the realign event behavior, so it is important to verify the event feature layer's reassign event behavior configuration before checking the Reassign to abandoned route check box.\nThe calibrate event behavior is applied before the realign event behavior, so it is important to verify the event feature layer's calibrate event behavior configuration before checking the Recalibrate route downstream check box.\nTo explore other event behaviors for reassignment and calibration, refer to the Event behavior for route reassignment and Event behavior for route calibration topics.", "section": "Note:"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#7EB

## 路線調整事件行為 · 調整后路線

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-256

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#FMB

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　由于向廢棄路線的改派受到檢查,調整后區域內的路線和部分路線已成為新的廢棄路線,位于稱為B線的一條線上的一條線上。 2路-Ab路有17條起步措施,22條結束措施,3路-Ab路有25條起步措施,35條結束措施,4路-Ab路有38條起步措施,43條結束措施,這些措施來自調整后的路線上的原有措施。2號公路是調整后區域的唯一路線,因為調整后的區域從2號公路開始,2號公路在下游進行檢查,2號公路在下游重新校準,它將原來的第4號公路退役,2號公路的新措施根據調整時間長短決定。下表顯示參與路線編輯的編輯活動及其相應的事件行為:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Since Reassign to abandoned route is checked, the routes and the portions of routes that are within the realign region have become new, abandoned routes on a line named Line B. Route 2_Ab has a start measure of 17 and an end measure of 22. Route 3_Ab has a start measure of 25 and an end measure of 35. Route 4_Ab has a start measure of 38 and an end measure of 43. These measures are from the original measures on the realigned routes.\nRoute 2 becomes the only route in the realignment area, as the realignment starts on Route 2. Since Recalibrate route downstream is checked, Route 2 is recalibrated downstream, and it retires the original Route 4. The new measures of Route 2 are determined by the length of realignment.\nThe following table shows the edit activities involved in the route edit and their corresponding event behaviors:", "section": "Routes after realignment"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#FMB

## 路線調整事件行為 · 保持事件行為

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-269

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#866

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　雖然事件保持了地理位置,但措施可以改變。如果事件跨過調整區域,事件也可以分開。當 Snap被設定為重置事件行為時,在調整區域內的事件和部分事件將按放棄的路線發生,以維持其地理位置。上述路線調整具有以下效果:1號事件在調整之日退出,因為其中的一部分在編輯部分。然后它分成兩個事件記錄,在調整后路線上,與調整日期作為起始日期。由于重新配置事件行為在調整事件行為之前應用,因此第1號事件的一部分在調整部分中與原事件1分開,與廢棄路線相交。另一個是,在調整后活動行為之前適用。事件 1 中 在 A 線上停留的部分 縮短為 A 線左部分 。 當應用 調整 停止 事件 時 , 該事件不需要改變 維持 地理位置 的措施 。 結果, 事件 1 分成兩個事件 。 其中 一 事件 留在 A 線上, 起始量為 0 , 尾量為 17 號 。 另一 事件 與 被遺棄 的 路線 相撞 , 起始量為 17 號 , 起始數為 2_ Ab 路 , 終點數為 30 號 , 尾量為 3_ Ab 路 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Although the geographic location of the event is maintained, the measures can change. The event can also split if it crosses the realign region. When Snap is set as the reassign event behavior, the events and portions of events that are within the realign region will snap to the abandoned route to maintain their geographic locations.\nThe route realignment described above has the following effects:\nEvent 1 is retired on the date of realignment since part of it is in the edit section. It then splits into two event records on the postrealignment route with the realignment date as the starting date. As the reassign event behavior is applied before the realign event behavior, the portion of Event 1 that is in the realignment section splits from the original Event 1 and snaps to the abandoned routes. The other portion of Event 1 that stays on Line A is shortened to the length of the left portion of Line A before the realign Stay Put event behavior is applied. When the realign Stay Put event behavior is applied, this event does not need to change its measures to maintain its geographic location. As a result, Event 1 splits into two events. One event stays on Line A with a start measure of 0 on Route 1 and an end measure of 17 on Route 2. The other event snaps to the abandoned routes with a start measure of 17 on Route 2_Ab and an end measure of 30 on Route 3_Ab.", "section": "Stay Put event behavior"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#866

## 路線調整事件行為 · 保持事件行為

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-270

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#866

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　事件 2 在調整當日退出, 因為部分在編輯欄中。 由于重指派事件行為首先應用, 活動 2 的一部分從原始事件 2 分離出來, 切換到廢棄路線 。 當校準保持布置行為被應用時, 無法創建活動 2 的下游 。 因為路徑 2 已經在此位置取代了第四號路 。 因此, 事件 2 只有一個新事件記錄, 從3_ Ab 第30度開始, 到 4_ Ab 路第43度結束 。 以下圖表顯示調整后的路線和事件 :

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Event 2 is retired on the date of realignment because part of it is in the edit section. As the reassign event behavior is applied first, the portion of Event 2 that is in the realignment section splits from the original Event 2 and snaps to the abandoned routes. The other portion of Event 2 that was downstream of realignment cannot be created when the calibrate Stay Put behavior is applied, because Route 2 has replaced Route 4 at this location. As a result, there is only one new event record for Event 2 that starts from measure 30 on Route 3_Ab and ends at measure 43 on Route 4_Ab.\nThe following diagram shows the routes and events after realignment:", "section": "Stay Put event behavior"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#866

## 路線調整事件行為 · 在一個橫跨事件的直線網絡中進行不放棄放棄情景的調整

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-277

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#09C

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　以下各節描述上述路線調整活動的重新調整封面和“吸附”事件行為。檢查了重新校準路線下游復選框,但沒有檢查改派到廢棄路線。因此,調整區內的路線將退出,而不會被重新劃為放棄線。事件與校準定時事件行為配置在一起。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The following sections describe the realign Cover and Snap event behaviors for the route realignment activity above. The Recalibrate route downstream check box is checked but Reassign to abandoned route is not checked. As a result, routes in the realignment area will retire without being reassigned to a line of abandonment.\nThe events are configured with the calibrate Stay Put event behavior.", "section": "Realignment without abandonment scenario in a line network with spanning events"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#09C

## 路線調整事件行為 · 注:

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-278

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 資料資料資料資料資料資料
- 線條參照
- 編輯編輯
- 事件行為

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#09C

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　校準事件行為在調整事件行為之前應用, 所以在檢查 Recalibrate 路由下游復選框前, 必須先驗證事件特征層的校準事件行為配置。 要查看校準的其他事件行為, 請參考用于路線校準主題的事件行為 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The calibrate event behavior is applied before the realign event behavior, so it is important to verify the event feature layer's calibrate event behavior configuration before checking the Recalibrate route downstream check box.\nTo see other event behaviors for calibration, refer to the Event behavior for route calibration topic.", "section": "Note:"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#09C

**text**　路線2成為調整區的唯一路線,因為調整開始在路線2上。由于對下游的重新校準路線進行了檢查,2號公路是重新校準后下游的,它將原路線4退去。2號公路的新措施由調整期長短決定。由于未對改派到廢棄路線進行檢查,因此沒有建立廢棄路線。下圖顯示調整后的路線:下表提供調整后路線的詳細情況:

No abandoned route is created as Reassign to abandoned route is not checked.\nThe following diagram shows the routes after realignment:\nThe following table provides details about the routes after realignment:", "section": "Routes after realignment"}

## 路線調整事件行為 · 封面事件行為

**id**　0710fddbb6e9bd0d85-286

**english**　Event behavior for route realignment

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#099

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html

**text**　如果事件在調整區, 則其形狀會按比例改變以覆蓋路線的調整部分。 該事件的測量也更新以反映新形狀。 對于調整下游的事件, 將應用校準事件行為 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "If the event is in the realignment area, its shape is changed proportionally to cover the realigned portion of the route. The measures of the event are also updated to reflect the new shape. For events in the downstream portion of realignment, calibrate event behavior is applied.", "section": "Cover event behavior"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/data/linear-referencing/event-behavior-for-route-realignment.html#099
